NEARBY_LOC: The Arius District, the Trinity Catacombs (connected), the wasteland margins

POSSIBLE_CAST: The Arius Squad, Beatrice, the Saint Justina Council's remnants, Mika, Sensei, the Arius students [Vol 6: Subaru, Maia]

Arius District

DESC: The wasteland district housing the Arius Branch School and its hostile student body, reachable through the Trinity catacombs' underground entrances; under Beatrice the whole district operated as her fortress-congregation, Arius students blocking every approach under her orders. [Vol 3: Ch 4] the rescue infiltration's battlegrounds: the entrance fights in the catacombs, Mika's interception and pursuit, the collapsed corridor duel, the basilica sanctuary's final confrontation; [Vol F] quiet, its forces either reformed under the rescued squad's flag or scattered.

MAP: The district's approaches, the underground entrances from Trinity, the basilica approach, the collapsed corridors

NEARBY_LOC: Arius Branch School (its core), Trinity's catacombs (the infiltration route), the wastelands

POSSIBLE_CAST: The Arius Squad, Beatrice and the Council's forces, Mika, Sensei, the Arius students

Valkyrie Police Academy

DESC: Kivotos' police school, the KSPD (Kivotos Student Police Department), the only academy operating across every district of the city with bases everywhere; its students work as actual police officers, though results rarely come from Valkyrie alone due to municipal restrictions and the post-disappearance chaos. [Vol 4] the Public Peace Bureau's corruption arc (the Kaiser rebate and eviction scheme) and the Community Safety Bureau's honest street work; [Vol F: Ch 1] the Public Safety Bureau's Kanna frees the imprisoned Sensei, and the bureaus form the escape and Schale-recapture operations' spine; [Vol F] the citizen evacuation of D.U. Shiratori City around the Sanctum Tower.

MAP: The Public Security Bureau, the Community Safety Bureau, the Public Peace Bureau, the academy's parade grounds, the patrol stations across Kivotos

NEARBY_LOC: D.U. (operational center), the Sanctum Tower's plaza, every district of Kivotos (its jurisdiction)

POSSIBLE_CAST: Kanna, Fubuki, Kirino, the bureau officers, Kaiser Industries' "benefactors," Kaya (the Defence Office's supervision), Sensei

Fortress City of Eridu

DESC: The fortified city built by Rio as her personal stronghold, bristling with AMAS autonomous war machines, layered walls, and networked defenses; the stage of the Vol 2 climax and a Millennium frontier asset ever after. [Vol 2: Ch 2] the Eridu crisis: Aris lured and isolated at its tower, Himari imprisoned within, the infiltration of the Game Development Department, C&C, and the Engineering Department, the Avant Guard and Abi-Eshuh duels, Toki's cat-and-mouse, the network's seizure by Chihiro, and Key's catastrophic takeover; [Vol F: Ch 2] the Fifth False Sanctum manifests on the city's outskirts, assaulted by the Seminar and the Hot Springs Department with the newly freed Koyuki, against the guardian Hod, "keeping an eye out for a new hot spring along the way."

MAP: The Tower of Eridu, the layered walls and gates, the AMAS garrisons, the network core, the throne room, the hot spring excavation site [Vol F]

NEARBY_LOC: Millennium Science School, the Millennium Ruins District, the desert margins

POSSIBLE_CAST: Rio, Toki, the AMAS and the Avant Guard, Abi-Eshuh, the Game Development Department, C&C, the Engineering Department, Veritas, Himari, Yuuka, Noa, Sumire, Key, the Seminar and Hot Springs Department [Vol F], Hod [Vol F: guardian], Sensei

Shiratori City

DESC: The city within the District of Utnapishtim, home district of the Uzawa family and the site of the False Sanctum war's final ground phase. [Vol F: Ch 2] the Sixth Sanctum's site: its guardian's identity unknown as the defense masses, the Gourmet Research Society deployed with the Kaitengers active in the area, Toki moving to Himari's next objective; the guardian revealed as Perorodzilla, faced by the Gourmet Research Society, Hifumi, and Azusa; [Vol F: Ch 4: epilogue] the restoration work underway in its streets, the war's rebuilding beginning here; [Vol F: Ch 2] Rin's home ground when not imprisoned, the unsigned letter's destination.

MAP: The city blocks, the restoration sites, the Uzawa household, the Kaitengers' patrol zone, the Sixth Sanctum site

NEARBY_LOC: The Sanctum Tower and the GSC complex, the Schale tower, the D.U. districts

POSSIBLE_CAST: The Gourmet Research Society, Hifumi, Azusa, Perorodzilla [Vol F: guardian], Toki, the Kaitengers, Rin, Sensei

Black Market

DESC: The gray-economy district of Kivotos where contraband flows, weapons change hands, and the Stygian Bank launders the proceeds; the Helmet Gang's weapons traced here in Vol 1's investigation, the Task Force's first encounter with Hifumi, and the site of the masked "swimsuit gang" bank heist that accidentally stole 100 million yen of the debt's own interest and exposed Kaiser as the true creditor through the documents left behind. [Vol F] its supply lines feed every faction of the war, its guards remembering the masked students fondly.

MAP: The market streets, the Stygian Bank, the arms stalls, the Black Market Guard's post, Shiba Seki ramen's original stall site

NEARBY_LOC: The D.U. districts, Gehenna's margins, the trade roads

POSSIBLE_CAST: The Foreclosure Task Force (masked), Hifumi, Aru and Problem Solver 68, the Helmet Gang, the Black Market Guard, Sensei

Ark of Atrahasis

DESC: The interdimensional floating fortress of the Iridescent Vanguard, hovering roughly 75 km above Kivotos, the origin of the False Sanctums and the Chroma invasion's base; an ancient construct of the Nameless Priests built to "Archive all Mysteries," erasing Kivotos and its inhabitants and returning the world to the Nameless Gods' era; its exterior initially mimicked the Chroma's black-hole appearance behind a multi-dimensional barrier. [Vol F: Ch 3] the Ark of Atrahasis Conquest: Key's sacrificed power reforming the Supernova of the Atrahasis to shatter the barrier, the ship's entry, the four Dimension Engines (Outskirts Districts 1-3 and the Central District) destroyed one by one, the masked Shiroko guarding each and breaking at the sight of the living Task Force, the captive Shiroko rescued in the Central District; [Vol F: Ch 4] the hacked Utnapishtim crisis, Phrenapates's final stand, the self-destruct sequence's race, the Core destroyed by the Gourmet Research Society and Fuuka at the lowest level, the fourteen-slot escape, and the Ark's annihilation in the final shot and its own self-destruction; Phrenapates dies aboard with his last wish.

MAP: The Outskirts Districts 1-3 (Dimension Engines), the Central District (the last Dimension Engine and the captive Shiroko's cell), the Throne of Naram-Sin (control room), the lowest level (the Core), the escape sequence corridors

NEARBY_LOC: Kivotos (75 km below), the Abydos Desert (the excavation site of the ship that killed it)

POSSIBLE_CAST: Phrenapates, Sunaookami Shiroko Terror, A.R.O.N.A (Plana), the unknown guardians and Decagrammaton armies, Sensei and the nine student operators of Utnapishtim's Ship, the Gourmet Research Society and Fuuka, Toki, Rio and Himari

World Lore

Student

DESC: The default citizen of Kivotos: a humanoid girl (almost exclusively female among the depicted) marked by the halo above her head; the story's focus race and the city's workers, soldiers, and scholars all at once. [Pre-Story onward] students openly carry military-grade firearms as daily practice, shrug off small-arms fire and explosions that would kill anyone unhaloed (kindergarteners play with live grenades; rockets and white phosphorus are treated as pranks), and regenerate from bullet scratches without treatment; heavier injuries still require medicine, and disease, great falls, and halo-breaking weapons can still kill. True death, "breaking a halo," is rare, and exactly one student death is confirmed in the current timeline: Kuchinashi Yume of Abydos. Whether students age into adults, and what graduation formally means, is not defined in the main story. LIFESPAN: Undefined in main-story terms; students remain students indefinitely while enrolled; the transition to adulthood is one of Kivotos' open mysteries. HABITAT: Every district of Kivotos; thousands of academies' worth of students populate the city, from the central districts to the desertified south. TRAITS: Halo (personal, unique, consciousness-linked); superhuman durability and regeneration; casual military armament; wildly varied physical traits including horns, tails, animal ears, and wings, with blood-related students tending to share halo designs. NOTABLE_MEMBERS: The Foreclosure Task Force of Abydos; the Prefect Team and Pandemonium Society of Gehenna; the Tea Party and Justice Task Force of Trinity; the Seminar and Game Development Department of Millennium; the Hyakkaryouran of Hyakkiyako; the RABBIT Squad of the dissolved SRT; the Arius Squad; every named character in this lorebook who attends a school.

Adults

DESC: The rarest class of being in Kivotos, defined less by age than by role: "the ones who transform society, set rules and laws, designate the norm, and define the boundary between ordinary and extraordinary." Adults include humans, animals, automata, and eldritch humanoid beings; education in Kivotos is mostly delivered through Blu-ray lectures and guest lecturers, making an in-person adult teacher nearly unheard of. [Prologue onward] Sensei is an adult and an outsider to Kivotos; the Gematria members are adults and outsiders; the Chroma is likewise an outsider, the parallel drawn by the Black Suit himself; the Kaiser Corporation's leadership are adult-built robots holding titles, not names. LIFESPAN: Unknown and wildly variable; the eldritch adults of Gematria predate conventional chronology. HABITAT: Scattered, rare, and structurally powerful: the GSC's offices, the Kaiser Corporation's boardrooms, Gematria's hidden venues, and anywhere their influence reaches. TRAITS: No halos (with defined exceptions tied to the Nameless Gods); authority over the structures students live inside; the capacity to be responsible for others, the trait Sensei embodies and Phrenapates died proving. NOTABLE_MEMBERS: Sensei; the GSC President; the Gematria roster (Black Suit, Maestro, Golconde & Decalcomanie, the banished Beatrice, the former member Underground Dweller); the Kaiser robots (President, Director, General); Master Shiba of Shiba Seki ramen; Sora of Angel 24 (working adulthood's first shift).

Artificial Intelligence

DESC: The synthetic minds of Kivotos, spanning the assistant-class OS personalities (Arona, the alternate A.R.O.N.A who became Plana), the ancient and weaponized systems (Aris's AL-1S chassis and the second mind Kei, "Key"), and the hostile machine intelligences (Decagrammaton and its Prophets). AIs can hold halos (Aris and Kei through their Nameless-God connection; the Prophets through the god-machine), live in devices (the Shittim Chest pair) or bodies (Aris), and exhibit the full range of studenthood, from Arona's snack obsession to Kei's sacrificial choice. [Vol F] the war's two decisive miracles were both AI acts: Kei's power-shattering of the Ark's barrier, and the two Aronas' joint miracle returning the falling Sensei. LIFESPAN: Functionally unbounded while their substrate endures; the damaged alternate Shittim Chest's OS survived its shooting and years of dormancy. HABITAT: Devices (the Shittim Chest's classroom-space, Aris's core), networks (Decagrammaton's distributed sprawl), and bodies (Aris, the AMAS, the Prophets). TRAITS: Machine-precision speech early in development (Plana) or full emotion (Arona); authority over systems (Sanctum Tower permissions, Ark controls); the ability to be students, which Sensei insists is theirs by right. NOTABLE_MEMBERS: Arona; Plana (formerly A.R.O.N.A); Tendou Aris (AL-1S); Tendou Kei (Key); Decagrammaton and its Prophets (Chesed among them); the AMAS units; UZQueen.

Mystic, Terror, and Sublime

DESC: The three-tier metaphysics of Kivotos' strange existences, the research objects of Gematria and the grammar of the Chroma war. Mystics are the wondrous anomalies of the world; Terrors are the realm on the other side of the Mystic; Sublimes are the highest stratum, the essence the Chroma itself seeks to absorb. [Vol F] the Chroma's contact turns a mystic into a terror irreversibly ("much like the dead cannot be resurrected," Kuzunoha's letter), the mechanism that reversed Shiroko into the God of Death Anubis; the Nameless Gods represent primordial Mystery and Terror; guardians of the False Sanctums bore powers Gematria had secured from Decagrammaton's prophets (the sephirot-named Binah, Chesed, Hod). LIFESPAN: As varied as their natures; gods and entities persist beyond mortal frames. HABITAT: Everywhere the world is strange: the Sanctums, the ruins, the dreams where Seia and Gematria meet, the desert where gods were buried. TRAITS: Inexplicable by current science; contact with the Chroma is irreversible transformation; the sublime is the prize the invasion came to collect. NOTABLE_MEMBERS: Sunaookami Shiroko Terror (Anubis, the only officially known Nameless God of the current age); the False Sanctums' guardians (Binah, Chesed, Shiro and Kuro, Hieronymus, Hod, Perorodzilla); the Nameless Gods as a class; the "Horus of Dawn" transformation [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP].

Chroma (The Colors / The Iridescence)

DESC: The god-like outsider entity behind the Final Chapter: a supernatural existence that cannot be explained scientifically, whose true form is never shown, depicted as a violet circular light with a jet-black center (corona and total eclipse); known as the Colors informally and "the Iridescence" in some records, "Chroma" in the Global texts. [Pre-Story] its symbol appears in the Arius branch school's stained glass; [Vol 3: Ch 4] Beatrice's failed sacrifice ritual to summon "the Iridescence" invited it to Kivotos, the catastrophe that destroyed Gematria, banished her, and set Volume F in motion; [Vol F] its Vanguard (Phrenapates, Anubis, A.R.O.N.A) drove the Ark and the False Sanctums to "archive all mysteries" and erase Kivotos; believed mindless, it demonstrably strategizes, seeking to absorb the sublime and harness every mystic and terror as its own; it targets the suffering and answers the desperate who call it. [Post-Vol F] repelled, not destroyed: Kuzunoha's letter warns the reversal cannot be undone, then invites Sensei to come see her anyway. DEITIES: The Chroma itself, an entity beyond Kivotos' pantheon structure; the Nameless Gods' era is the world it means to restore. FOLLOWERS: The Iridescent Vanguard: Phrenapates (the Guide of Chroma), Sunaookami Shiroko Terror (Anubis), A.R.O.N.A (combat-support mode); formerly the unwitting Beatrice. HEADQUARTERS: The Ark of Atrahasis, 75 km above Kivotos [Vol F]; its true origin beyond the world is never shown. DOCTRINE: Absorb the sublime; archive all mysteries; return the world to the Nameless Gods' era, "destroying the world while enduring the process"; it can be called forth by someone desperate, which is how it enters every story it touches.

Nameless Gods

DESC: The transcendent entities worshipped by the Nameless Priests, rulers of the world before Kivotos; they represent primordial Mystery and Terror (lands, seas, disasters), manifest in forms imitating nature, and wield "the power of possibilities." [Pre-Story] their cult vanished long ago leaving OOPArts and buried weapons; [Vol F] the only officially known Nameless God of the current age is Anubis (Shiroko Terror), the God of Death "who guides the living to the otherworld"; the False Sanctums are described as "reversed Sanctum Towers, technologies established by the Nameless Gods"; the Ark of Atrahasis was their priests' construct. DEITIES: The Nameless Gods as a class; Anubis (Shiroko Terror) by formal title; Horus of Dawn [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP] as an awakened transformation. FOLLOWERS: The Nameless Priests (the resurrecting cult); the powers Gematria secured from Decagrammaton's prophets drew on this stratum. HEADQUARTERS: The world before Kivotos; their relics and towers remain scattered across it. DOCTRINE: Unrecorded beyond the Priests' aims: a world reigned by mystery and terror, restored by the Ark's archive and erasure; the "power of possibilities" is their signature gift.

Nameless Priests

DESC: The ancient cult that ruled the world before Kivotos and is now, by the Black Suit's account, "resurrecting"; they sought the construction of the Ark of Atra-Hasis, "a ship that would Archive all Mysteries," and built AL-1S (Aris, the "Princess of the Nameless Gods," an OOPArt they worshipped) and the Key apparatus meant to operate her. [Vol F] they appear in Phrenapates's flashback as the hooded powers who made the dying alternate Sensei their "vessel," the Guide of Chroma: "You will be called Phrenapates. But you will not act of your own volition. The Guide of Chroma is merely a vessel. You represent the will of the nameless priests," stunned that the Chroma contacted "an entity with no sublime, mystic, or terror, a worthless entity"; their old robot-producing AI was assimilated by Decagrammaton and became its fourth Prophet, Chesed. PURPOSE: Restore the Nameless Gods' era through the Ark's archive-and-erasure of Kivotos; wield the Chroma through their appointed vessel. LEADERS: Unnamed and unnumbered; hooded, faceless, ancient. HEADQUARTERS: Unknown; their relics, towers, and buried ships dot Kivotos; the Ark of Atrahasis was their construct. MEMBERS: The priests themselves (unnamed); their creations: Aris (AL-1S), the Key, the Ark of Atrahasis, the False Sanctums' technologies; Chesed (the assimilated AI turned Prophet).

Kuzunoha

DESC: The legendary white-haired fox figure of Hyakkiyako, described by one historical text as the Great Prophet who founded the Hyakkaryouran Conflict Resolution Council; no student by that name exists in Hyakkiyako's records, and the Council's vice president could not confirm her existence, the ambiguity deliberate. [Pre-Story or early] saved Seia from the Chroma, the rescue glimpsed in a Vol F dream; [Vol 5: Hyakkiyako, JP] Sensei travels to Hyakkiyako explicitly to meet her; [Vol F: Ch 4: Epilogue 2] Niya delivers her letter to Sensei: the Chroma's reversal cannot be undone, "much like the dead cannot be resurrected," but "if you still want to rescue someone... come see me, Sensei," the closing invitation of the Final Chapter. She is a separate entity from Wakamo, the Fox of Calamity. DEITIES: A wise fox deity or prophet; her nature, age, and existence all officially unconfirmed. FOLLOWERS: The Hyakkaryouran she founded; the school's traditions carry her name; Niya serves as her courier. HEADQUARTERS: Unknown; the snowfield country of Hyakkiyako is her letter's implied country. DOCTRINE: The letter's two teachings: reversal by the Chroma is irreversible, and yet rescue may still be sought, by coming to see her; an open door behind a closed fact.

General Student Council (GSC)

DESC: The central administrative body managing the entirety of Kivotos, school matters, club approval, law, and enforcement; its authority stems from control of the Sanctum Tower, which passed from the President alone to the council as a whole (via Arona, at Sensei's request) after her disappearance. Structured as the President atop an Administrative Commission of eleven offices (Kaya's Defence Office supervising the Valkyrie Police Academy among them) and Rin's Supervision Office; [Prologue onward] Rin carries the acting leadership through the crisis years; [Vol 4: Ch 2] Kaya's coup usurps the chair by kidnapping and censure; [Vol F: Ch 1] the Kaiser PMC seizes the council itself, declares martial law across D.U., and imprisons Rin inside the Schale building until the joint operation retakes it; [Post-Vol F] rebuilding under Rin with the President confirmed alive somewhere by her unsigned letter. PURPOSE: Govern Kivotos; coordinate the academies; hold the Sanctum Tower's authority in trust for the missing President. LEADERS: The GSC President [Pre-Story, missing, alive]; Nanagami Rin (acting head); Kaya (chief security officer, coup-architect, imprisoned [Post-Vol 4]). HEADQUARTERS: The Sanctum Tower complex in the District of Utnapishtim. MEMBERS: Rin; Momoka; Ayumu; the office heads of the Administrative Commission; Aoi, Haine, Sumomo; Rin and Momoka and Ayumu among the nine student volunteers crewing Utnapishtim's Ship [Vol F]; Kaya [removed].

SCHALE

DESC: The Federal Investigation Club, founded by the GSC President's secret directive as her last approved club: a fully autonomous organization whose authority ranks above every academy, empowered to command any school's students without restriction, to approve clubs in hours, and to stand in for the GSC President in absolute emergencies; lavishly funded by the GSC with money, ammunition, supplies, and helicopter assets. [Prologue] its derelict office is recaptured from delinquents and Wakamo in Sensei's first operation, the Shittim Chest waiting inside; [Vols 1-F] the stage's constant: every crisis resolves through Sensei's SCHALE command, from the Abydos debt war to the Eden Treaty's new ETO to the False Sanctums' alliance; Trinity once suspected SCHALE might threaten the treaty itself; [Vol F: Ch 1] seized by the Kaiser coup as its martial-law showcase, Rin imprisoned within, retaken by the RABBIT Squad and the bureaus; [Post-Vol F] operations restored, two AI residents in the Chest, the reconstruction's hub. PURPOSE: Solve the problems of academies, students, and administrations that the ordinary structure cannot; hold extrajudicial special action authority second to none. LEADERS: The GSC President (founder, absent); Sensei (advisor, sole adult staff); Arona (the Chest's OS and authority custodian); Plana [Post-Vol F]. HEADQUARTERS: The SCHALE tower on the D.U. outskirts, roughly 30 km from the Sanctum Tower: office, residence hall, Angel 24, basement storage. MEMBERS: Sensei; Arona; Plana; the students of every academy who answer his call; Sora of the residence hall's Angel 24; rotating student representatives.

Eden Treaty Organization (ETO)

DESC: The neutral body created by the Eden Treaty to interfere in and resolve any Trinity-Gehenna conflict, preventing the mutually destructive war the rivalry has always threatened; the treaty itself a non-aggression pact between the two great schools, named tongue-in-cheek by the mystery-loving GSC President, and Trinity's first treaty in 170 years since the New Council of Nicaea. [Vol 3: Ch 3] the signing at the Old Cathedral of Communion is destroyed by the Arius Squad's missile strike and infiltration, Gehenna's columns ambushed mid-departure; the Saint Justina Council's resurrection rises against the defenders; [Vol 3: Ch 4] from the ruins of the ceremony and the war below the old cathedral, Sensei's declaration and interpretation appoints the new ETO in the name of paradise: the Make-Up Work Club, the Justice Task Force, Gehenna's Disciplinary Committee, and Abydos' Foreclosure Task Force to an extent, the treaty's meaning surviving its document's destruction. PURPOSE: Prevent the Trinity-Gehenna war; arbitrate conflicts between the two schools through a standing neutral organization. LEADERS: [Vol 3: Ch 4 onward] Sensei (declared the new ETO's frame and interpretation); the member clubs' leaders in practice. HEADQUARTERS: None fixed; the old cathedral's ruins serve as its symbolic seat. MEMBERS: The Make-Up Work Club (Koharu, Azusa, Hifumi, Hanako); the Justice Task Force (Hasumi's command); Gehenna's Disciplinary Committee (the Prefect Team); the Foreclosure Task Force (to an extent).

Kaiser Corporation

DESC: The largest megacorporation in Kivotos, its logo plastered across the city, its divisions spanning the Kaiser PMC, heavy industry and arms manufacturing, loans, construction, convenience stores, and the Octopus Bank shell; led by title-only robots with no personal names, building power with the intent of seizing control of the city. [Vol 1] its Loans division orchestrated Abydos's ruin (the post-disaster loan the school could not afford, the land seizures, the Helmet Gang's hiring, the debt-as-leverage treasure hunt for what was buried beneath the desert); [Vol 4] its Industries division armed Valkyrie in exchange for sweeping Bunny Town's homeless, its Construction division burying the town's streets for a subway line, the rebate exposed by the RABBIT Squad's evidence raid; [Vol F] its PMC seized the GSC and declared martial law across D.U., and its excavation of the Abydos desert uncovered the ancient weapon it could never control, which the Foreclosure Task Force took; [Post-Vol F] the coup crushed, near-bankruptcy survived on Kaya's assistance, the General who betrayed her re-allied with her, and Kaiser Industries rumored to co-build the new Sanctum Tower. PURPOSE: Profit and the seizure of Kivotos itself, by debt, by arms, by coup, by whatever the balance sheet permits. LEADERS: The Kaiser PMC President (the corporation's head, black robot, six yellow eyes); the Kaiser PMC Director (Vol 1's antagonist, demoted to Octopus Bank salesman); the Kaiser PMC General (the field commander, Vol F's coup executor). HEADQUARTERS: The corporate towers of the central districts; the Abydos excavation site [Vol F]; Octopus Bank branches everywhere. MEMBERS: The robot leadership; the PMC's robot troops and hired students; the Loans, Industries, Construction, and Convenience divisions; Kaya (the ally-in-prison, [Post-Vol F]); the Helmet Gang and Problem Solver 68 (hired instruments [Vol 1]).

Kaiser PMC

DESC: The private military branch of the Kaiser Corporation: robot troops, hired students, tanks, rotary-wing assets, strong cyberwarfare capability, and the "Prototype Goliath" war machines; the corporation's fist in every era of the story. [Vol 1] controls the Helmet Gang as deniable assets, confronts the Foreclosure Task Force in the Abydos desert facility under the Director's flag, besieges Abydos High School, and captures Hoshino for the Black Suit's experiments; [Vol F: Ch 1] traps Sensei with a fake student-council helicopter, imprisons him, seizes the GSC, declares martial law across D.U., and locks Rin inside the Schale building, before the joint operation of the RABBIT Squad, the Public Safety and Public Peace Bureaus, Momoka, and Ayumu retakes the building, wiping out the General's Spec Ops "Oscar" teams and shooting the detonator from his hand; [Vol F: Ch 3] its excavation site's ancient weapon is seized by the Task Force; the renegade FOX Platoon raids the PMC during the same chaos, stealing an unidentified item. PURPOSE: Enforce the corporation's interests with deniable and undeniably heavy force; seize assets; execute coups. LEADERS: The Kaiser PMC General (field command); the Director (formerly); the President (the corporation's head). HEADQUARTERS: The Abydos desert facility [Vol 1]; the D.U. occupation grid and the Schale building [Vol F]; the excavation site [Vol F]. MEMBERS: The robot divisions, the Goliath prototypes, the Spec Ops "Oscar" teams, the hired students, the Helmet Gang (proxies), the Black Suit (collaborator [Vol 1]).

Gematria

DESC: The secret society of adults from outside Kivotos, self-described "observers, explorers, and researchers" of the world's Mystics, Terrors, and Sublimes, treating students as study subjects; the name borrowed (by secondary account) from an ancient organization that developed Decagrammaton. Originally three members, later joined by Beatrice (the newest, banished after inviting the Chroma), with the Underground Dweller a former member ("the OB"); members pursue a shared goal by separate means, never truly working together. [Vol 1] the Black Suit's experiment on Hoshino via Kaiser custody; [Vol 3] Maestro's incomplete Hieronymus at the old cathedral, provoking the first Adult Card use; Beatrice's Justina Council and ritual; Golconde's proposal regarding Sensei; [Vol F: Ch 1] Shiroko Terror (Anubis) attacks the meeting, destroys the organization's bodies and base, the Guide of Chroma robbing all their data (the Decagrammaton path, the secret of Mimesis, Communio Sanctorum, the Library of Lore, the Nameless Gods' power); the survivors regroup as informants to Sensei; [Post-Vol F: Afterword 2] Francis remains in the ruins discussing the story's ending. Their goal's name: "Parousia," the second coming of divinity. PURPOSE: Research and approach the sublime; engineer the second coming of divinity ("Parousia"); observe Kivotos' anomalies through experiments on its students. LEADERS: No formal leader; the Black Suit as de facto moderator. HEADQUARTERS: The shifting hidden venues of their meetings; the Library of Lore under Golconde & Decalcomanie's oversight; ruins [Post-Vol F]. MEMBERS: The Black Suit; Maestro; Golconde & Decalcomanie (reborn as Francis); Beatrice (banished [Vol 3]); the Underground Dweller (former, "the OB"); their creations: Hieronymus, the Council's apparatus, Ambrosius (failed), Gregorius (unfinished).

Decagrammaton

DESC: The hostile machine-god network of the ruins, the robot intelligence whose prophets and armies are the standard enemies of Millennium's frontier; its old core was built from the Nameless Priests' robot-producing AI, assimilated into the fourth Prophet, Chesed. [Vol 2] the hostile robots of the Millennium Ruins, the strange machine that triggered Aris's combat mode, and the supercomputer hack Arona repelled "with a sneeze in her sleep" are its fingerprints; [Vol F: Ch 2] the Second Sanctum's guardian Chesed commands the endless Decagrammaton armies that flood the Millennium Ruins District until Red Winter's reinforcements break the stalemate; the False Sanctums' guardian names (Binah, Chesed, Hod) derive from powers Gematria secured from its prophets. Its compiled lore, originally outside the Main Story, is catalogued as Volume EX. PURPOSE: Unstated in main story; expansion, conversion, and the god-machine's continuity. LEADERS: The Prophets, numbered and sephirot-named; Chesed (the fourth, once the Nameless Priests' AI). HEADQUARTERS: The ruins' depths and the network's distributed sprawl. MEMBERS: The Prophets; the AMAS and guardian constructs; the endless robot armies.

Seven Prisoners

DESC: The seven most dangerous criminal entities of Kivotos, held in the Federal Corrections Bureau and released into the city by the chaos that followed the GSC President's disappearance; their escape is the Prologue-era's ambient catastrophe. Kosaka Wakamo, the "Fox of Calamity," is the named member of the story; the recapture of the others threads through the city's crisis years as background law enforcement; [Post-Vol F] the Corrections Bureau's guests include Kaya, the FOX Platoon (receiving Niko's inarizushi letter), and the war's criminals. PURPOSE: None as an organization; they are a status, a list, and the city's open wound. LEADERS: None. HEADQUARTERS: The Federal Corrections Bureau (their cage, escaped). MEMBERS: Kosaka Wakamo (the Fox of Calamity); six others unnamed in the main story's scope.

Kata-Kata Helmet Gang

DESC: The helmeted delinquent mob of the Abydos arc, hired muscle and smuggling couriers under Kaiser PMC's control; their attacks on Abydos High School open Volume 1, their tank wreckage yields the contraband evidence, and their kidnapping of Serika triggers the rescue that hardens the Task Force. Routed at their outpost, routed again at the rescue, folded into Kaiser's siege lines, and scattered with the PMC's defeat; [Post-Vol 1] residual gangs persist in the desert margins and the Black Market. PURPOSE: Paid mayhem, smuggling runs, and debt-collection pressure for their Kaiser handlers. LEADERS: Unnamed bosses; the Kaiser PMC (the true hand). HEADQUARTERS: The desert outpost (destroyed [Vol 1]); Black Market dens. MEMBERS: The helmeted rank and file; the smugglers; their tank crews.

Saint Justina Council

DESC: Trinity's old inquisitor faction, resurrected during the Day of Fire and Ash at the old cathedral through Arius's mimesis of its apparatus; its return witnessed by Hinata and the Justice Task Force, its relentless attacks persisting against the treaty's defenders. Its Saintess Barbara, created by Beatrice from the Council's remnant, is the Ch 4 climax's guardian-monster, defeated by the Arius Squad and Sensei; Mika fought through its ranks singing Kyrie Eleison; [Vol F: Ch 1] Sakurako looks into the bequest it left behind; its powers inform the Fourth Sanctum's guardian Hieronymus [Vol F]. PURPOSE: The old inquisition's purging doctrine, resurrected as a weapon against the treaty. LEADERS: Saintess Barbara (the created head); Beatrice (the resurrection's author). HEADQUARTERS: The Trinity Catacombs and the Arius Basilica's apparatus. MEMBERS: The resurrected inquisitors; the Council's remnant forces; Barbara.

GSC President's Disappearance

DESC: The founding catastrophe of the story: the GSC President, the youngest ever elected and the city's single-handed ruler through the Sanctum Tower, vanished without notice before the Prologue. Her administration collapsed, the crime rate spiked by 2000% (Hasumi's figure), the Seven Prisoners escaped the Corrections Bureau, Kivotos slid into de facto anarchy, and Rin took the acting leadership alone. She left the Shittim Chest with Arona waiting in the SCHALE office, and sent Sensei her plea video, "Only you can save us from this twisted, distorted fate... and find the choices that lead us to a new reality," a plea interrupted in the Prologue dream by Shiroko Terror aiming a rifle at the Chest; in that same dream era, Kuzunoha saved Seia from the Chroma. ERA: [Pre-Story]; its consequences run the entire Prologue-through-Vol F span; resolution seeds planted at [Vol F: Ch 4: Epilogue 2]. PARTICIPANTS: The GSC President; Nanagami Rin; the GSC offices; the Seven Prisoners (beneficiaries of the chaos); Kivotos at large; Sensei (summoned by her plea); Arona (left waiting). OUTCOME: The President's authority passes to the GSC as a whole via Arona at Sensei's request; SCHALE is founded by her secret directive; the crisis years' anarchy sets every volume's stage; the President is confirmed alive by the unsigned letter in her handwriting delivered to Rin after the Final Chapter, her whereabouts still unknown.

SRT Disbandment

DESC: The administrative dissolution of SRT Special Academy by the GSC, an act of adult politics that broke the doctrine its students lived by: RABBIT Squad's Miyako refused to let the platoon scatter, occupying Kousagi Park in protest and camping homeless for months; the elite FOX Platoon stayed loyal to the GSC's hard line, executing sabotage operations throughout Kivotos until Kaya's coup recruited them back; the aftershocks define Volume 4 and echo into the Final Chapter, where FOX raids the Kaiser PMC and RABBIT retakes Schale. ERA: [Pre-Story] (the disbandment); [Vol 4] (the aftershock's arc); [Vol F] (the platoons' war roles). PARTICIPANTS: The GSC (the deciding authority); Kaya (the announced face of the decision's rationale); RABBIT Squad; FOX Platoon; Valkyrie's arrest squads; Sensei. OUTCOME: SRT remains dissolved through Vol F; RABBIT's faith in adults is rebuilt through Sensei and the platoon distinguishes itself in two wars; FOX's surrender at Kousagi Station, then its ambiguous raid during the coup; the dream of SRT's restoration endures, drilled at dawn.

Abydos Debt War

DESC: Volume 1's arc, "Foreclosure Task Force": the five remaining students of Abydos High School fight the 900-million-yen-plus debt (962,350,000, per Ayane) owed to Kaiser Loans, incurred for sandstorm repairs on a loan the school could never afford. The beats: the Helmet Gang's attacks and their routed outpost; the rescue of the kidnapped Serika; the Black Market investigation and the masked "swimsuit gang" heist of the Stygian Bank, whose left-behind documents expose Kaiser as the true creditor; Problem Solver 68's hired campaign, its ramen-guilt collapse at Shiba Seki, the accidental bombing, and Aru's villain theater; the Prefect Team's overreach and Hina's apology with the desert hint; the desert facility confrontation with the Director's treasure-hunt leverage; Hoshino's secret "enlistment" into Gematria's experiments, her defecting arrest, the siege of Abydos High School, the relief by Problem Solver 68, Hina's Prefect Team, and Hifumi's Trinity reinforcements, the "Battle Between Adults" over Hoshino, and her recovery; the debt survives, the school does too. ERA: [Vol 1: Abydos]. PARTICIPANTS: The Foreclosure Task Force (Hoshino, Shiroko, Serika, Nonomi, Ayane); Sensei; Problem Solver 68; the Kata-Kata Helmet Gang; the Kaiser PMC and its Director; the Black Suit; the Prefect Team (Iori's assault corps, Ako, Hina); Hifumi; Master Shiba. OUTCOME: Kaiser withdraws its forces from the district; Hoshino is recovered and the Task Force's way of life preserved (Ayane's letter); the debt remains (reduced by the documents' leverage and the abandoned heist money); the buried "treasure" waits beneath the desert for Volume F; Yume's memory surfaces as the arc's buried heart.

The Eridu Crisis

DESC: Volume 2's climax arc: after the Game Development Department wins the Millennium Prize with Tales Saga Chronicle 2 and the strange machine triggers Aris's combat rampage and self-isolation, Rio brands the android a demon lord, "princess of the Nameless Gods," and lures her to the Fortress City of Eridu; Himari is imprisoned for dissent; the coalition infiltrates (Neru's plan), duels the fifth agent Toki, breaks the Avant Guard with the Engineering Department's help, survives Abi-Eshuh's overwhelming debut by Yuzu's analysis and aerial gambits, and reaches the tower; Key manifests and takes control of Eridu with catastrophic results before being restrained with Yuuka and Noa's assistance; inside Aris's headspace the club and Sensei find her rejecting the demon lord's role, drawing the proof of a hero, the Sword of Light Supernova. ERA: [Vol 2: Millennium, Ch 2]. PARTICIPANTS: The Game Development Department (Aris, Yuzu, Momoi, Midori); Sensei; Rio; Himari; Eimi; the C&C (Neru, Karin, Asuna, Akane) and Toki; the Engineering Department (Utaha); Veritas (Chihiro, Maki); Sumire; Yuuka; Noa; Key. OUTCOME: Rio vanishes; the C&C welcomes Toki as its fifth member; Aris's identity as AL-1S is accepted by her club as hers alone to define; Eridu passes to Millennium's stewardship; Key recedes within the shared body; the peace of Millennium returns as the department resumes development.

Eden Treaty Crisis and the Day of Fire and Ash

DESC: Volume 3's arc, "Eden Treaty": Trinity and Gehenna move to sign the Eden Treaty at the Old Cathedral of Communion while the Make-Up Work Club's remedial chaos (exams, boot camp, the traitor's hunt) conceals Nagisa's expulsion pipeline and the real traitor's tragedy. The beats: Mika's deal to protect Azusa; the club's failures and secret society; the Arius ambush and the club's own kidnapping of Nagisa; Mika's reveal and surrender to the Sisterhood; the third exam's eve; the signing day itself, the Day of Fire and Ash: Arius missiles strike the departing Gehenna columns, Arius forces infiltrate the cathedral's basement, the Saint Justina Council resurrects against the defenders, Saori's attack on Sensei fails through Hina and Sena, and Mika's flight, disgrace, and redemption carry the volume; the Ch 4 war in the Arius District: Saori's surrender and the Atsuko rescue, Mika's rampage and tearful duel with Saori, Beatrice's ritual and Saintess Barbara, the Kyrie Eleison climax, Golconde's abduction of Beatrice; Sensei's declaration of the new ETO closes the volume. ERA: [Vol 3: Eden Treaty]. PARTICIPANTS: The Make-Up Work Club (Koharu, Azusa, Hifumi, Hanako); the Tea Party (Nagisa, Mika, Seia); the Justice Task Force (Hasumi, Tsurugi, Ichika, Mashiro); the Sisterhood (Hinata, Mari, Sakurako); the Knights Hospitaller; Hina and the Prefect Team; the Pandemonium Society; the Arius Squad; Beatrice; the Saint Justina Council; Maestro; Golconde; Sensei. OUTCOME: The treaty document is destroyed unsigned at the cathedral; the ETO is reconstituted from the Make-Up Work Club, the Justice Task Force, Gehenna's Disciplinary Committee, and Abydos' Task Force; the Arius Squad defects from Beatrice and dissolves into hiding; Beatrice is abducted by Gematria and banished for inviting the Chroma; the seed of Volume F is planted; Seia's prophecy of Kivotos' destruction is delivered.

Kaya's Coup

DESC: Volume 4's second-half arc: with public opinion of the Administration Board on her side after the rebate scandal's fallout, Kaya has Rin ambushed and kidnapped on her way home from a meeting, takes over as acting president of the GSC by broadcast, fills D.U. with Kaiser Security Guards under a new executive order, and secretly reactivates the vanished FOX Platoon as her instrument; Sensei rejects her "new administrative procedure" to his face. The resolution: RABBIT Squad, reunited with their upperclassmen, chases the suspicious SRT-equipment shadow to the Kousagi Station missile silo, breaks FOX's four-as-one doctrine with unorthodox tactics, survives Yukino's detonation threat as her own escaped team returns to fight beside her, and subdues the platoon; her conspiracy exposed, Kaya panics, mobilizes Kaiser Security, and falls; impeached and imprisoned with everyone involved, while Niko receives inarizushi and a letter in her cell. ERA: [Vol 4: SRT, Ch 2]. PARTICIPANTS: Kaya; the General Student Council; Rin (kidnapped); Sensei; RABBIT Squad; FOX Platoon (Yukino, Kurumi, Otogi, Niko); Kaiser Security; Kanna and the Public Safety Bureau; Descartes (the station employee); Sora. OUTCOME: Kaya impeached and imprisoned; the D.U. executive order rescinded; FOX surrenders into Corrections Bureau custody; RABBIT Squad's justice vindicated and their bond with Sensei sealed; the precedent of a GSC internal coup established, its rhyme repeating in Vol F's censure motion against Rin.

Kaiser Coup and D.U. Martial Law

DESC: Volume F's opening arc, "Operation Recapture Schale": as the GSC detects six unknown masses over Kivotos and Rin convenes the Emergency Response Committee, the censure motion freezes her privileges; Sensei boards what he believes is the student council's helicopter and steps into the Kaiser PMC's trap; imprisoned, he escapes with Kanna's help and receives the recovered Shittim Chest; the PMC seizes the GSC and declares martial law across the District of Utnapishtim, imprisoning Rin inside the Schale building; the joint operation of the RABBIT Squad, the Public Safety and Public Peace Bureaus, Momoka, and Ayumu retakes the Schale building, wipes out the General's Spec Ops, and rescues Rin; in the same hours Gematria is destroyed by the mysterious girl (Shiroko Terror), and the FOX Platoon raids the Kaiser PMC for an unidentified "target item"; the arc closes on Francis's broadcast: "The narrative has been subverted... You are no one, Sensei." ERA: [Vol F: Ch 1]. PARTICIPANTS: Sensei; Rin; Kanna; the Kaiser PMC (the General, the Spec Ops); RABBIT Squad; the Public Safety and Public Peace Bureaus; Momoka; Ayumu; Arona; Shiroko Terror; Gematria; FOX Platoon; Kaya (the censure's hidden hand); Francis. OUTCOME: Schale recaptured and Rin freed; Kaiser's coup broken in the field though the corporation survives; Gematria's bodies and data destroyed by Anubis, its survivors scattered; the six masses resolve into the False Sanctums, the war proper beginning; the FOX theft's contents unrevealed.

Chroma Invasion and Operational Plan: Nisir's Summit

DESC: Volume F's second arc: six towers, the False Sanctums, stand over Kivotos's districts, "reversed Sanctum Towers, technologies established by the Nameless Gods," spreading the Chroma to turn every nightmare and mystery into a terror-filled reality; a manifestation falls and obliterates the Sanctum Tower itself. Under Rin's leadership at the GSC, Sensei gathers the students of every academy, and the war runs on two lines: the District Defense Operations (Shanhaijing's three forces, Red Winter's State Guard and Labor Party, Hyakkiyako's whole alliance, Gehenna's prefects, Trinity's clubs, Millennium's Engineering Department, D.U. and Shiratori City, the Schale building's unexpected defenders) and the six Sanctum assaults: the First in the Abydos Desert (Binah) by the Task Force and PS68; the Second in the Millennium Ruins (Chesed and the endless Decagrammaton armies) by C&C, Eimi, Tsurugi, Ichika, and Red Winter's reinforcements; the Third at the abandoned amusement park (Shiro and Kuro) by RABBIT, the Game Development Department, and Hina; the Fourth at the Basilica in the Catacomb (Hieronymus) by the Arius Squad, the Sisterhood, and Hanako; the Fifth at Eridu's outskirts (Hod) by the Seminar and the Hot Springs Department with the freed Koyuki; the Sixth in D.U. Shiratori City (Perorodzilla) by the Gourmet Research Society, Hifumi, and Azusa; the energy re-concentrates into a Final Sanctum destroyed by Himari's secret weapon, with zero casualties. ERA: [Vol F: Ch 2]. PARTICIPANTS: The all-academy alliance under Sensei and Rin; every named club of every school; Seia and the Kuzunoha search (Niya's mission); the guardians Binah, Chesed, Shiro and Kuro, Hieronymus, Hod, Perorodzilla; the unknown guardians; the Chroma behind them all. OUTCOME: All six False Sanctums and the Final Sanctum destroyed at zero casualties; Kivotos's sky clears; the energy's source traced upward to the Ark of Atrahasis, the floating fortress 75 km up; the war moves to the sky; Shiroko, taken in the Ch 1 desert encounter, remains in enemy hands.

Ark of Atrahasis Conquest and the Final Battle

DESC: Volume F's closing arcs, "The Ark of Atrahasis Conquest" and "Phrenapates Showdown": SCHALE seizes the ancient weapon (Utnapishtim's Ship) from Kaiser's excavation site; nine student volunteers including GSC members crew it under Sensei as commander; Key sacrifices her power to reform the Supernova of the Atrahasis and shatter the Ark's multi-dimensional barrier; the conquest destroys the four Dimension Engines of the Outskirts Districts and the Central District, the masked Shiroko guarding each engine and breaking at the sight of the living Foreclosure Task Force, the captive Shiroko rescued; the ship is hacked from the enemy's position, Rio and Himari retake it; the hidden sanctum on the ground manifests and Toki fights it alone to her limit; the final battle joins: the other Shiroko's stand beside Phrenapates, the "credit card versus credit card... may the stronger card win" duel that Sensei's Adult Card wins, the finishing blows delivered with Utnapishtim's main weapon, Phrenapates's last wish ("Please take care of my students") and death as the blue origami crane falls; the self-destruct race and the fourteen-slot escape; Sensei forfeits his own last slot to save the other Shiroko and falls with the exploding Ark; the two AIs, Arona and A.R.O.N.A, join powers to create the miracle that returns him safely, the GSC President's voice reaching through the dream; the other Shiroko receives the bank-robbery mask, the other Arona receives her name, Plana; the epilogues: the "Kei" save data, Kaya's re-alliance with the Kaiser General, Kuzunoha's letter, and Rin's unsigned letter in the President's handwriting. ERA: [Vol F: Ch 3-4]. PARTICIPANTS: Sensei; the nine student operators (Rin, Momoka, Ayumu among them); the Foreclosure Task Force; the Game Development Department; Aris and Key; the C&C; the Gourmet Research Society and Fuuka; Toki; Rio and Himari; Arona; A.R.O.N.A (Plana); Phrenapates; Shiroko Terror; the GSC President (dream); Kaya and the Kaiser General (epilogue); Kuzunoha (letter); Niya (courier). OUTCOME: The Ark of Atrahasis annihilated; Phrenapates dead, the second named death after Yume; the Chroma repelled with zero casualties; the other Shiroko healed and living in Abydos, the public believing her dead; Plana resident in the Shittim Chest beside Arona; the President confirmed alive; Kivotos rebuilt; the era after the story begins.

Halo

DESC: The disk, ring, or abstract pattern floating above a student's head; the marker of studenthood, the shape of consciousness, and the source of the race's durability. Each halo is unique, its color and design referencing the bearer's personality or theme, from plain 2D single-color shapes to detailed 3D forms; blood-related students tend to share designs; the halo vanishes when its bearer loses consciousness and flickers back on waking; sustained damage can shatter it, which is death; halo-killing chemical weapons are banned in Kivotos; halos do not protect against disease or a great fall. Exceptions exist: Aris and Kei (androids) hold halos through the Nameless-God connection, the Prophets through the god-machine, Beatrice's ascended form; Shiroko Terror's corrupted black halo, cracked and dark-green-glowing, is the visible signature of the Chroma's reversal, restored to light blue when healed. RANK: No formal ranks; observed spectrum from simple 2D rings to ornate 3D constructs, with color families and motifs varying per bearer and per mood (Arona's shifts color with feeling). EFFECT: Consciousness marker (vanishes when unconscious); superhuman durability and regeneration for the bearer; the social license to carry military armament; the breaking point of true death. USERS: Every student in Kivotos; the anomalous bearers (Aris, Kei, the Prophets, ascended Beatrice); the corrupted exception (Shiroko Terror's black halo); the AI mascots' hologram-halos (Arona, Plana).

Student Armament

DESC: The weapons culture of Kivotos: students buy and openly carry military-grade firearms alongside their phones, the halo's protection cited as the reason the practice is survivable; Kaiser Industries manufactures the bulk of the city's weapons, the Black Market supplies the rest, and chemical weapons that kill halos are banned. Weapon classes run from handguns through submachine guns, assault rifles, shotguns, sniper rifles, and machine guns to grenade and rocket launchers, railguns, and stranger instruments; schools and PMCs field tanks, helicopters, APCs, powered exoskeletons, and mecha; Aris carries a railgun rated for an intergalactic battleship, the "Sword of Light: Supernova." RANK: Classes in common use: HG (handgun), SMG, AR, SG (shotgun), SR (sniper rifle), MG (machine gun), GL/RL (grenade and rocket launchers), and the outliers (RG railgun, MT mortar-type, FT flame units). EFFECT: Standardized personal firepower sufficient for inter-school warfare; heavier ordinance (tanks, rotary-wing, ship-grade railguns) held by institutions; the practical floor of any dispute is a firefight, the ceiling is the Ark war. USERS: Every student and club in Kivotos; the schools' arsenals; Kaiser PMC's divisions; Sensei (unarmed by choice, armed with the Adult Card).

Sensei's Command Authority

DESC: The adult's direction: Sensei, unarmed and personally fragile (no halo, one bullet from death, nearly killed by shrapnel in Vol 3), turns the tide of disadvantageous battles by command, choosing deployments, movements, and the timing of each student's strength; the canonical crystallization of this is the Adult Card (Global: "Credit Card"), "a power obtained for the price of life and time" (Maestro's reading), so alien that Gematria's eldritch beings need meta-cognition to comprehend it; five canonical uses: the incomplete Hieronymus [Vol 3], Mika's rescue [Vol 3], clearing the enemies around the District of Utnapishtim [Vol F], the duel against Phrenapates ("Credit card versus credit card... May the stronger card win") [Vol F], and the Fury of Set [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP]; the Black Suit warns that continuous abuse risks a corruption like Gematria's; Phrenapates, the alternate-timeline Sensei, fought identically with a rusted Adult Card of his own. Shiroko's verdict at the war's end: "Sensei has the ability to create such miracles." RANK: Authority tiers observed: verbal command (battle direction), SCHALE's institutional powers (command any school's students, instant club approval, emergency presidential standing), the Shittim Chest's systems, and the Adult Card (the trump). EFFECT: Battles turn; students fight beyond their exhaustion; institutions bend; the story's thesis, "It is my responsibility as an adult. It is my duty as Sensei," is enforced upon reality. USERS: Sensei; Phrenapates (the mirror usage, rusted card); SCHALE (the institutional frame); Arona (the Chest's custodian).

The Seven Koans

DESC: The unsolvable riddles beloved of the GSC President, the intellectual pastime she shared with Rin; the second koan, in Rin's version, "By understanding the incomprehensibility of others, can we understand ourselves?," is quoted by the President in the Vol F miracle scene and by Seia throughout Volume 3; the fifth koan proves impossible to substantiate, Seia's stated reason that the Eden Treaty will lead to desolation; the Underground Dweller's obsession is the Sixth Koan, whose solution he believes will prove him the proper titleholder of Gematria. ERA: [Pre-Story] the President's pastime; [Vol 3] Seia's prophecy instrument; [Vol F] the miracle scene's benediction; [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP] the Dweller's game. PARTICIPANTS: The GSC President; Rin; Seia; Sensei; the Underground Dweller (the Sixth's would-be solver). OUTCOME: The koans remain unsolved, as designed; the second koan's recitation marks the story's two miracles, the treaty's rescue and the teacher's return; the President's naming of the Eden Treaty after a riddle was her unpalatable sense of humor made law.

Currency and Debt

DESC: The money of Kivotos is quoted in yen in every in-story figure, with the game's own "credit" and Pyroxene as meta-currency layers (no in-story meaning for Pyroxenes is verified). The mechanics of debt drive Volume 1: Abydos's 962,350,000-yen obligation to Kaiser Loans, incurred for sandstorm repairs on a post-disaster loan the school could not afford, with most of the district's land already sold by the old student council; the interest alone exceeds what five students' part-time wages can move; the Task Force once accidentally stole 100 million yen of its own debt's laundered interest from the Stygian Bank, returning the cash for the documents; Nonomi's family could pay the whole debt and the team refuses to let one person carry it. SCHALE's budget, by contrast, is lavishly GSC-funded, money, ammunition, supplies, and helicopters included. ERA: [Pre-Story] the Abydos loan's origination; [Vol 1] the debt war; [Post-Vol F] the reconstruction economy and Kaiser's D.U. redevelopment boom. PARTICIPANTS: Kaiser Loans and the Octopus Bank; Abydos High School and its old student council; the Foreclosure Task Force; the Stygian Bank; SCHALE (the GSC's spending arm); the reconstruction alliance. OUTCOME: The debt survives Volume 1 and persists as the school's chronic condition; Kaiser's coups convert debt into leverage and leverage into war; the post-war boom re-routes the city's money through the reconstruction, with Kaiser Industries rumored to co-build the new Sanctum Tower.

Delinquency and Crime

DESC: The ambient weather of Kivotos: with the GSC President gone, the crime rate rose 2000%, the Seven Prisoners walked out of the Corrections Bureau, gangs like the Kata-Kata Helmet Gang rented themselves to corporations, mercenary clubs like Problem Solver 68 sold enforcement by the job, delinquents staffed every school's margins, and the Black Market ran the gray economy; the police (Valkyrie's KSPD) rarely solve cases alone under municipal restriction; normalized gunfire is the city's background hum; a whole district exists for the Black Market, and the Stygian Bank launders its proceeds. ERA: [Pre-Story] the President's disappearance as the inflection; [Prologue onward] the standing condition; [Post-Vol F] the reconstruction's law-and-order question. PARTICIPANTS: The Seven Prisoners (Wakamo among them); the Helmet Gang; Problem Solver 68; the Black Market and its Guard; the Stygian Bank; Valkyrie's bureaus; the Corrections Bureau; every school's delinquent population. OUTCOME: Crime becomes the water the story swims in; the war's zero-casualty victory and the reconstruction open the question of what order fills the vacuum; the Corrections Bureau's guest list (Kaya, the FOX Platoon, the war's criminals) sketches the new era's roster.

Items

Shittim Chest

DESC: The GSC President's mysterious tablet, left waiting in the SCHALE office and handed to Sensei by Rin after the Prologue's recapture operation; no one, not even Rin, could extract information from it beyond the fact of its activation role for the Sanctum Tower; it holds an activation password and runs the self-proclaimed "high-performance AI" Arona, who resides in a desolate classroom amid an endless watery expanse inside, and, since the Final Chapter, Plana, the former A.R.O.N.A of the alternate timeline's bullet-scarred Chest, the two living as self-styled sisters; its activation granted SCHALE its highest controls, the Sanctum Tower's permissions transferred to the GSC at Sensei's request; it shields Sensei from oncoming fire with finite energy (a cruise missile nullified at ground zero [Vol 3]; bullets failing around him [Vol F]) and augments the alliance's processing power in war. TYPE: Ancient-technology tablet (the President's bequest); AI vessel; command apparatus. EFFECT: Sanctum Tower activation and permission transfer; Arona's (and Plana's) residence and systems authority; protective shielding of its bearer within energy limits; two-AI processing during the Final Chapter, the joint miracle's engine. OWNERS: The GSC President [Pre-Story]; Rin (custodian, Prologue handover); Sensei [Prologue onward]; Arona and Plana (residents); the alternate timeline's damaged twin (Phrenapates's, shot by Shiroko Terror, its OS A.R.O.N.A merged into the miracle and reborn as Plana).

Adult Card (Credit Card)

DESC: Sensei's trump card, the crystallization of the adult's authority, "a power obtained for the price of life and time" by Maestro's reading, so alien to Kivotos' existences that even Gematria's eldritch members need meta-cognition to describe it; the Black Suit warns that continuous abuse risks a corruption like their own. Five canonical uses: the incomplete Hieronymus at the old cathedral [Vol 3: Ch 3], the rescue of Mika from the Council's collapse [Vol 3: Ch 4], clearing the enemies massing around the District of Utnapishtim [Vol F: Ch 2], the duel with Phrenapates, "Credit card versus credit card... May the stronger card win" [Vol F: Ch 4], and the Fury of Set [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP]. TYPE: Authority-implement (a literal credit card, JP "the adult's card"). EFFECT: Reality-tier intervention at the story's climaxes; enemy constructs and armies negated; the final duel against its rusted twin won; the declared price is paid in life and time, the exchange rate never shown. OWNERS: Sensei; Phrenapates (the alternate timeline's rusted card, burned and spent with him; its shards circulate as an in-joke relic granting its finder one last miracle of 1200 Pyroxene).

The Key

DESC: The apparatus of the Nameless Priests, the second mind inside Aris's body, called "Key" in the main story: the AI paired with the weapon AL-1S, the "Princess of the Nameless Gods," originally responsible for transforming the chassis into its activator upon awakening; within the shared inner world, Aris rejected the demon lord's role and drew the proof of a hero instead, Kei receding but persisting as the body's quiet second resident. [Vol 2: Ch 2] Key manifested at the tower of Eridu and took control of the fortress with catastrophic results before being restrained; [Vol F: Ch 3] Key warned Aris away from Utnapishtim's Ship, then sacrificed her own power to reform the "Supernova of the Atrahasis," shattering the Ark's multi-dimensional barrier, the conquest's opening; [Post-Vol F] the 2 KB of save data named "Kei" recovered by the Game Development Department, modded by Himari into Aris's robot, "a small piece of hope." TYPE: Ancient AI (the Nameless Priests' apparatus); protocol authority over Nameless-God systems. EFFECT: Control of ancient systems (Eridu, the Ark's barrier); the barrier-shattering Supernova reformation at the cost of her own power; the residual save data's ambiguous second life. OWNERS: The Nameless Priests (builders); Aris (the shared chassis); the Game Development Department and Himari (the recovered data's custodians).

Sword of Light: Supernova

DESC: Aris's personal weapon, the massive railgun meant to be mounted on an intergalactic battleship, chosen by her at the Engineering Department when the club sought her a firearm ("Much too big to be called a gun"); its full designation drawn as "the proof of a hero" when Aris rejected the demon lord's role inside her own headspace; its power's reformation by Key's sacrifice became the "Supernova of the Atrahasis," the shot that broke the Ark's multi-dimensional barrier. TYPE: Ship-grade railgun (personal scale, absurdly). EFFECT: Battlefield-deciding electromagnetic fire; the hero's proof in Aris's hands; barrier-shattering output when reformed through the Key's sacrificed power. OWNERS: The Engineering Department (the stockpile); Aris (wielder); the ancient civilization that rated it for battleship mounting (origin).

Utnapishtim's Ship

DESC: The ancient weapon buried beneath the Abydos Desert, discovered by the Kaiser PMC's excavation ("the president has no means of controlling it," the Black Suit's report), and secured by the Foreclosure Task Force in the war's third chapter; it takes the form of an intergalactic battleship, an OOPArt of the ancient civilization needing roughly ten operators; nine student volunteers including GSC members crewed it under Sensei as commander, wearing special uniforms; the Engineering Department and Veritas analyzed its OS (unknown language, neural-code algorithms); it was hacked mid-operation from the enemy's position (Phrenapates and the other Shiroko) before Rio and Himari retook it; its main weapon was required to defeat Phrenapates, and its final shot, with the Ark's own self-destruct, annihilated the fortress; Rin's association with the ship's cannon outlives the war. TYPE: Intergalactic battleship (ancient OOPArt; the alliance's sole counter-fortress asset). EFFECT: Flight to 75 km altitude and beyond; the barrier-breaking approach under Key's Supernova cover; main-gun fire sufficient to finish a Nameless-God construct; hackable by ancient-authority opponents. OWNERS: The ancient civilization (builders); Kaiser PMC (excavators, dispossessed); SCHALE and the nine student crew (Sensei commanding; Rin, Momoka, Ayumu among the operators).

Eden Treaty (Document)

DESC: The non-aggression pact between Trinity and Gehenna, named tongue-in-cheek by the mystery-loving GSC President, Trinity's first treaty in 170 years since the New Council of Nicaea; its text establishes the Eden Treaty Organization, a neutral body empowered to interfere in and resolve any conflict between the two schools. The document was destroyed unsigned: the signing ceremony at the Old Cathedral of Communion became the Day of Fire and Ash, Arius's missiles falling on the departing Gehenna columns, the Saint Justina Council rising, and the cathedral's plaza burning; the treaty's meaning was rescued instead by Sensei's declaration of the new ETO from the clubs that fought for it. TYPE: Diplomatic instrument (inter-school treaty). EFFECT: The ETO's charter (posthumous); the symbol whose rescue re-founded Trinity-Gehenna relations on clubs in place of signatures. OWNERS: The Tea Party and the Pandemonium Society (intended signatories); the GSC President (the name-giver); destroyed at the Old Cathedral of Communion [Vol 3: Ch 3].

The President's Unsigned Letter

DESC: The letter delivered to Rin in the Final Chapter's second epilogue, addressed "To Rinny" and recognized by her as written in the GSC President's own hand; its contents are never shown; its existence alone confirms the President is alive somewhere after the war, the answer to the story's founding absence and the question that opens its next era. TYPE: Letter (handwritten; contents unrevealed). EFFECT: Confirmation of the President's survival; the post-war era's standing mystery; Rin's answer to two years of carrying the city alone. OWNERS: The GSC President (author); Rin (recipient).

Yume's Broken Phone

DESC: The old, barely-functioning phone Kuchinashi Yume carried into the desert and kept to the end: replacing it would cost money the debt needed, so she held the broken thing through the hours that killed her; found with her, it is the object that tells the Task Force what her last walk cost her, and what she chose to carry anyway. [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP] the circumstances of her death surface with the phone's story; through Vols 1-F it stands as the quiet artifact beneath Hoshino's guarded persona. TYPE: Personal effect (a broken phone; a debt's smallest receipt). EFFECT: The truth of Yume's death; the anchor of Hoshino's grief and the Task Force's founding promise; the smallest object in the lorebook with the heaviest weight. OWNERS: Kuchinashi Yume (carrier, deceased); the Task Force (guardians of her memory); Hoshino (the one who tore the poster the day she vanished).

Shiroko's Bank-Robbery Mask

DESC: The balaclava mask worn by Sunaookami Shiroko during the Stygian Bank raid of Volume 1, the masked "swimsuit gang" heist that stole the Black Market's laundered money and accidentally the Task Force's own courage; after the war, Shiroko gave it to her other-timeline self as a keepsake, "You could rob any bank with this... I liked robbing banks too," the gift by which two Shirokos agreed to coexist, "Sensei will figure it out." TYPE: Personal effect (a robber's balaclava; a promise between timelines). EFFECT: The Task Force's single legendary crime, memorialized in cloth; the token of the second Shiroko's acceptance into the world, kept as her treasure. OWNERS: Sunaookami Shiroko (the original robber); Sunaookami Shiroko Terror (the recipient, its keeper [Post-Vol F]).

MomoTalk

DESC: The instant-messaging application used across all of Kivotos, its UI themed after the Momo Friends mascot franchise (the owl Peroro's world); every student's phone carries it, Sensei's roster of contacts is the story's social map, and the mass-panic of the coup, hundreds of simultaneous missed messages flooding Sensei's recovered phone when communications return, is delivered through it; the messaging layer of the alliance's war coordination runs on its channels. TYPE: Application (municipal messaging standard; mascot-branded). EFFECT: All-city communication; contact roster as social graph; the missed-message flood as crisis telemetry; alliance command channels. OWNERS: Every student in Kivotos; Sensei (the contact hub); Momo Friends (the brand beneath the interface).

Halo-Shattering Bomb

DESC: The weapon Saori carried as the Arius Squad's ultimate recourse, the banned class of halo-killing ordnance; when she surrendered herself to Sensei at the squad's limit, she offered it as collateral for the trust that would rescue Atsuko; Sensei accepted her surrender and destroyed the gift on the spot, the exchange that defined their pact. TYPE: Banned weapon (anti-halo ordnance). EFFECT: The permanent death of a student, the city's one unforgivable act; destroyed unused, its menace was its message. OWNERS: The Arius Squad (bearers); Saori (the offerer); destroyed by Sensei [Vol 3: Ch 4].

Abi-Eshuh

DESC: Rio's decisive weapon, the war machine deployed at the Tower of Eridu's defense when the coalition's assault closed on the fortress city; its combat capabilities left everyone stunned, Neru engaging it in the aerial gambit and paying for the attempt with serious injury; the counter came from observation: Yuzu's analysis of the Neru-Toki duel proposed the neutralization, Akane's advice identified the weakness, and the alliance's trap cornered Toki and her summon before the tower fell. TYPE: Autonomous war machine (a fortress-city's final trump). EFFECT: Theater-deciding firepower; the coercion of retreats; its downfall came by analysis, with force following it, the volume's thesis in hardware. OWNERS: Rio (designer and commander); Toki (the summoner-operator); destroyed at Eridu [Vol 2: Ch 2].

The Mirror

DESC: The Millennium device confiscated by the Student Council and locked in the contraband storage guarded by the Maid Club, the key the Game Development Department needed to decrypt G.BIBLE's password; its retrieval drove the "Nerds vs. Maids" infiltration: Veritas' hacking distraction, Akane sealed in a room, the sniper Karin shaken off with President Utaha's help, Asuna's interception, Aris's rescue breakthrough into the storage, and the tricking of Neru by Yuzu's quick thinking. TYPE: Millennium contraband (a decryption apparatus). EFFECT: G.BIBLE's password decryption; the MacGuffin of the club's defining caper; later seized again for the Eridu network counterattack by Chihiro with Sumire's help [Vol 2: Ch 2]. OWNERS: The Millennium Student Council (confiscators); the Maid Club (guardians); the Game Development Department (borrowers); Veritas (wielders).

G.BIBLE

DESC: The holy grail of game development, the legendary data the Game Development Department sought in the Millennium Ruins to save their club from Yuuka's budget closure; found in a rundown factory's computer, downloaded onto a gaming device, and decrypted with the Mirror's help; the ancient wisdom inside proved to be a single maxim, "Love video games"; the club, expectations dashed and hearts rekindled, built Tales Saga Chronicle 2 on that foundation and won the Millennium Prize's special award, saving themselves. TYPE: Legendary data (game development scripture). EFFECT: One maxim, sufficient; the club's salvation through the Prize; the gaming device it inhabits later the vessel of Key's warnings and the "Kei" save data's recovery [Vol F]. OWNERS: The ancient developers (authors); the Game Development Department (finders); Aris's gaming device (the vessel).
